# Era 5 Expanded Roadmap: v1901-v2000

**Theme:** Natural Conversation, Personality, and Relationship

This file expands every numbered version and subversion in the era. Browser work is continuous across these planning slots; a numbered checkpoint is a recoverable campaign save, not an automatic Codex handoff. Concrete evidence may justify a narrow repair, deferral, merge, or revised implementation while preserving the era outcome.

# Arc 17: Discourse and Conversational Flow

## Section A: Discourse state (v1901-v1908.9)

## v1901: Discourse state - Scope and evidence baseline

**Arc:** 17, Discourse and Conversational Flow  
**Section:** A, Discourse state  
**Technical dependency label (not handoff cadence):** WEB-BULK  
**Version objective:** Within `Discourse state`, define the exact observable problem, evidence sources, exclusions, and measurable outcome.  
**Parent outcome:** Model current question, topic, speaker intent, emotional context, unresolved references, and response obligations.

### v1901.0 - Evidence baseline
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Discourse state / Scope and evidence baseline`, Record the exact problem, attributable evidence, current behavior, target behavior, exclusions, and success metrics. Do not implement yet.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1901.1 - Contract
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Discourse state / Scope and evidence baseline`, Define schemas, invariants, provenance, lifecycle states, authority boundaries, compatibility rules, and failure states.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1901.2 - Foundations
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Discourse state / Scope and evidence baseline`, Implement the smallest portable deterministic core with focused unit fixtures and no live installation.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1901.3 - Integration
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Discourse state / Scope and evidence baseline`, Connect the foundation to established project boundaries without creating a second executor, memory store, or authority path.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1901.4 - Operator experience
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Discourse state / Scope and evidence baseline`, Add concise controls, status, receipts, correction, cancellation, and accessible presentation for the affected workflow.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1901.5 - Behavioral scenarios
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Discourse state / Scope and evidence baseline`, Add realistic end-to-end scenarios, including uncertainty, correction, mixed intent, and ordinary daily use.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1901.6 - Reliability
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Discourse state / Scope and evidence baseline`, Harden restart, replay, retry, timeout, cancellation, concurrency, stale state, and exactly-once behavior.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1901.7 - Adversarial review
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Discourse state / Scope and evidence baseline`, Test malformed, conflicting, deceptive, private, unauthorized, and scope-expanding inputs. Fail closed with useful guidance.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1901.8 - Reconciliation
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Discourse state / Scope and evidence baseline`, Reconcile performance, privacy, compatibility, documentation, release metadata, source immutability, and retained regressions.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1901.9 - Browser checkpoint
- **Owner:** BROWSER-CHECKPOINT (no Codex review required)
- **Deliverable:** For `Discourse state / Scope and evidence baseline`, Run the complete focused subsection suite and affected retained suites, update the cumulative ledger, and save a recoverable source-only candidate. Continue the campaign unless an exception gate is triggered.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.
